}\n\n    blocksMatching(state, scope, target) {\n        if (!state || !state.blocks) { return [] }\n        if (scope === 'block') {\n            return state.blocks.filter(function(b) { return b.id   if (Q.prosperity_magrail == null) {\n        Q.prosperity_magrail = PROSPERITY_IG_START.Magrail\n    }\n    if (Q.prosperity_brazos == null) {\n        Q.prosperity_brazos =  Q.stock_magrail = Math.round(98.8 * pm / scale * 10) / 10\n    }\n    var pb = Number(Q.prosperity_brazos)\n    if (!isNaN(pb)) {\n        Q.stock_brazos = Math.round(201.3 * pb / scale * 10) / 10\n    }\n    var pf = Number(Q.prosperity_families)\n    if (!isNaN(pf)) {\n        Q.cloud_9_street_price = Math.round(34.3 * pf / scale * 10) / 10\n    }\n    var pe = Number(Q.prosperity_edges)\n    if Q.overall_longshore * Q.trade_level\n    Q.affluent_longshore_rev = Q.overall_longshore * .41 - Q.overall_longshore * Q.trade_level\n    Q.corp_longshore_rev =  (Q.overall_longshore - Q.corp_longshore Q.corp_capital*.01 + (corp_spend *0.8 * Math.sqrt(Q.trade_level))\n\n    Q.corp_capital_revenue = corp_capital / 240\n}\n\nfunction coop_purchase(){\n    coop_spend = Q.coop_money / 36\n    Q.coop_spend\n\n}\n\n\nfunction economy_tick(){\n    trade_level_tick()\n    corp_purchase()\n}\n\n// Economic asset API: economic_assets_runtime.scene.dry\n\n\nvar CONTRACTING_VISITS_PER_PHASE = (Q.contracting_visits_remaining == null || isNaN(Number(Q.contracting_visits_remaining))) {\n        Q.contracting_visits_remaining = 0\n    }\n    return Number(Q.contracting_visits_remaining) || 0\n}\n\nfunction monthly_tick_phase1() {\n    syncContractingAllowance()\n    organize_tick()\n    trade_level_tick()\n    economy_tick()\n    applyStratumProsperityBumps()\n    if (typeof Q.processMonthlyHousing()\n    }\n    if (typeof Q.tabulateBudget === 'function') {\n        Q.tabulateBudget()\n    }\n    syncProsperityDisplays()\n}\n\n/** Add amount to every voter block in one Number(amount)\n    if (isNaN(bump) || bump === 0) { return false }\n    for (var i = 0; i < state.blocks.length; i++) {\n        var block = state.blocks[i]\n        if (block.district.id !== districtId) { continue }\n        block.prosperity = clampProsperity(\n            (block.prosperity ?? PROSPERITY_BLOCK_BASELINE) + bump\n        )\n    }\n    if (typeof Q.qified_block_prosperity === 'function') {\n        Q.qified_block_prosperity()\n    }\n    if (typeof Q.syncStratumProsperityAverages === 'function') {\n        Q.syncStratumProsperityAverages()\n    }\n    return true\n}\n\nQ.monthly_tick_phase1 = monthly_tick_phase1\nQ.applyStratumProsperityBumps = applyStratumProsperityBumps\nQ.bumpDistrictProsperity = bumpDistrictProsperity\nQ.applyProsperityConnections = applyProsperityConnections\nQ.syncProsperityDisplays = syncProsperityDisplays\n\ninitEconomyProsperity()\nif (typeof Q.initEconomicAssets === 'function') { Q.initEconomicAssets() }\nsyncProsperityDisplays()\n\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes() }\n\n!}"

'trade_level',\n    coefficient: 0.01,\n    strata: PROSPERITY_DOCKS_TRADE_STRATA,\n}\n\n/** crime_level excess → listed strata in Pitts only. */\nvar PROSPERITY_PITTS_CRIME = {\n    district: 'Pitts',\n    quality: 'crime_level',\n    coefficient: -0.012,\n    strata: {\n        first: 1, second: 1, third_wave: 1, coop: 1, deeper: 1, alien: 1,\n    },\n}\n\nfunction applyDistrictIntraProsperityMesh(state, blockDeltas) {\n    var districts = Q.prosperityDistrictIds || PROSPERITY_DISTRICT_IDS\n    var defaultMesh = Q.prosperityIntraDistrictMeshDefault || PROSPERITY_INTRA_DISTRICT_DEFAULT\n    var perDistrict = Q.prosperityIntraDistrictMesh || null\n\n    for (var d = 0; d < districts.length; d++) {\n        var districtId = districts[d]\n        var matrix = (perDistrict && perDistrict[districtId])\n            ? perDistrict[districtId]\n            : defaultMesh\n        var blocks = prosperityBlocksInDistrict(state, districtId)\n        if (blocks.length < 2) { continue }\n\n        for (var si = 0; si < blocks.length; si++) {\n            var source = blocks[si]\n            var fromSid = source.demographic.id\n            var signal = source.prosperity ?? PROSPERITY_BLOCK_BASELINE\n            for (var ti = 0; ti < blocks.length; ti++) {\n                var target = blocks[ti]\n                if (source.id === target.id) { continue }\n                var coeff = prosperityIntraMeshCoeff(\n                    matrix,\n                    fromSid,\n                    target.demographic.id\n                )\n                if (coeff === 0) { continue }\n                var weight = prosperityTransferMultiplier(\n                    source.population,\n                    target.population\n                )\n                var transfer = coeff * signal * weight\n                if (transfer === 0) { continue }\n                blockDeltas[target.id] = (blockDeltas[target.id] || 0) + transfer\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfunction applyIgHomeDistrictProsperityLinks(state, q, blockDeltas) {\n    var links = Q.prosperityIgHomeDistrictLinks || PROSPERITY_IG_HOME_DISTRICT_LINKS\n    for (var li = 0; li < links.length; li++) {\n        var row = links[li]\n        var igKey = 'prosperity_' + String(row.ig).toLowerCase()\n        var igVal = q[igKey]\n        if (igVal == null) { igVal = igProsperityDefault(row.ig) }\n        var signal = Number(igVal)\n        if (isNaN(signal)) { signal = igProsperityDefault(row.ig) }\n        var sourcePop = PROSPERITY_IG_EFFECTIVE_POP[row.ig]\n        if (sourcePop == null) { sourcePop = 20 }\n        var blocks = prosperityBlocksInDistrict(state, row.district)\n        var toStrata = row.toStrata || {}\n\n        for (var bi = 0; bi < blocks.length; bi++) {\n            var block = blocks[bi]\n            var sid = block.demographic.id\n            if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(toStrata, sid)) {\n                continue\n            }\n            var coeff = Number(toStrata[sid])\n            if (isNaN(coeff) || coeff === 0) { continue }\n            var weight = prosperityTransferMultiplier(sourcePop, block.population)\n            var transfer = coeff * signal * weight\n            if (transfer === 0) { continue }\n            blockDeltas[block.id] = (blockDeltas[block.id] || 0) + transfer\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfunction applyDocksTradeProsperityLinks(state, q, blockDeltas) {\n    var spec = Q.prosperityDocksTrade || PROSPERITY_DOCKS_TRADE\n    var excess = economyQualityExcess(q, spec.quality)\n    if (excess === 0) { return }\n    var sourcePop = prosperityDistrictTotalPop(state, spec.district)\n    if (sourcePop <= 0) { return }\n    var blocks = prosperityBlocksInDistrict(state, spec.district)\n    var strataCoeffs = spec.strata || {}\n\n    for (var bi = 0; bi < blocks.length; bi++) {\n        var block = blocks[bi]\n        var sid = block.demographic.id\n        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(strataCoeffs, sid)) {\n            continue\n        }\n        var strataMult = Number(strataCoeffs[sid])\n        if (isNaN(strataMult) || strataMult === 0) { continue }\n        var coeff = Number(spec.coefficient) * strataMult\n        if (isNaN(coeff) || coeff === 0) { continue }\n        var weight = prosperityTransferMultiplier(sourcePop, block.population)\n        var transfer = coeff * excess * weight\n        if (transfer === 0) { continue }\n        blockDeltas[block.id] = (blockDeltas[block.id] || 0) + transfer\n    }\n}\n\nfunction applyPittsCrimeProsperityLinks(state, q, blockDeltas) {\n    var spec = Q.prosperityPittsCrime || PROSPERITY_PITTS_CRIME\n    var excess = economyQualityExcess(q, spec.quality)\n    if (excess === 0) { return }\n    var sourcePop = prosperityDistrictTotalPop(state, spec.district)\n    if (sourcePop <= 0) { return }\n    var blocks = prosperityBlocksInDistrict(state, spec.district)\n    var strataCoeffs = spec.strata || {}\n    var baseCoeff = Number(spec.coefficient)\n    if (isNaN(baseCoeff)) { baseCoeff = 0 }\n\n    for (var bi = 0; bi < blocks.length; bi++) {\n        var block = blocks[bi]\n        var sid = block.demographic.id\n        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(strataCoeffs, sid)) { continue }\n        var strataMult = Number(strataCoeffs[sid])\n        if (isNaN(strataMult) || strataMult === 0) { continue }\n        var coeff = baseCoeff * strataMult\n        var weight = prosperityTransferMultiplier(sourcePop, block.population)\n        var transfer = coeff * excess * weight\n        if (transfer === 0) { continue }\n        blockDeltas[block.id] = (blockDeltas[block.id] || 0) + transfer\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * One-shot citywide prosperity grants per stratum via Q.<stratum>_bump (e.g. coop_bump).\n * Adds bump to every voter block in that class, clamps, syncs Q, then sets the key to 0.\n * Cards:  on-arrival: coop_bump = 0.05\n * Same month: call Q.applyStratumProsperityBumps() in on-arrival after setting bump.\n * Otherwise applied at the start of monthly_tick_phase1 (before connection bleed).\n */\nfunction applyStratumProsperityBumps() {\n    var state = Q.electionV2\n    if (!state || !state.blocks) { return false }\n\n    var strata = Q.stratum_labels || [\n        'rich', 'first', 'second', 'coop', 'drone', 'alien', 'deeper', 'third_wave',\n    ]\n    var changed = false\n\n    for (var s = 0; s < strata.length; s++) {\n        var sid = strata[s]\n        var key = sid + '_bump'\n        var raw = Q[key]\n        if (raw == null || raw === 0) {\n            Q[key] = 0\n            continue\n        }\n        var bump = Number(raw)\n        Q[key] = 0\n        if (isNaN(bump) || bump === 0) { continue }\n\n        for (var i = 0; i < state.blocks.length; i++) {\n            var block = state.blocks[i]\n            if (block.demographic.id !== sid) { continue }\n            block.prosperity = clampProsperity(\n                (block.prosperity ?? PROSPERITY_BLOCK_BASELINE) + bump\n            )\n        }\n        changed = true\n    }\n\n    if (changed && typeof Q.qified_block_prosperity === 'function') {\n        Q.qified_block_prosperity()\n    }\n    return changed\n}\n\nfunction applyProsperityConnections() {\n    var state = Q.electionV2\n    if (!state || !state.blocks || !Q.prosperityConnectionCatalog) { return }\n\n    initEconomyProsperity()\n\n    var blockDeltas = {}\n    var igDeltas = {}\n    var catalog = Q.prosperityConnectionCatalog\n\n    for (var i = 0; i < catalog.length; i++) {\n        catalog[i].accumulate(state, Q, blockDeltas, igDeltas)\n    }\n\n    applyDistrictIntraProsperityMesh(state, blockDeltas)\n    applyIgHomeDistrictProsperityLinks(state, Q, blockDeltas)\n    applyDocksTradeProsperityLinks(state, Q, blockDeltas)\n    applyPittsCrimeProsperityLinks(state, Q, blockDeltas)\n\n    for (var b = 0; b < state.blocks.length; b++) {\n        var block = state.blocks[b]\n        var delta = blockDeltas[block.id]\n        if (delta) {\n            block.prosperity = clampProsperity(\n                (block.prosperity ?? PROSPERITY_BLOCK_BASELINE) + delta\n            )\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (var igKey in igDeltas) {\n        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(igDeltas, igKey)) { continue }\n        var igVal = Q[igKey]\n        if (igVal == null) {\n            igVal = igProsperityDefault(igIdFromProsperityKey(igKey))\n        }\n        Q[igKey] = clampProsperity(igVal + igDeltas[igKey])\n    }\n\n    if (typeof Q.qified_block_prosperity === 'function') {\n        Q.qified_block_prosperity()\n    }\n}\n\nQ.ProsperityConnectionModifier = ProsperityConnectionModifier\n\n// Legacy rows (decay, card stacks). District mesh + home IG + Docks/Pitts: constants above.\nQ.prosperityConnectionCatalog = [\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_rich', 'stratum', 'rich', 'stratum', 'rich', -0.05, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_first', 'stratum', 'first', 'stratum', 'first', -0.05, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_second', 'stratum', 'second', 'stratum', 'second', -0.105, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_coop', 'stratum', 'coop', 'stratum', 'coop', -0.25, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_drone', 'stratum', 'drone', 'stratum', 'drone', -0.02, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_alien', 'stratum', 'alien', 'stratum', 'alien', -0.02, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_deeper', 'stratum', 'deeper', 'stratum', 'deeper', -0.02, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_third_wave', 'stratum', 'third_wave', 'stratum', 'third_wave', -0.05, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_soylent', 'ig', 'Soylent', 'ig', 'Soylent', -0.30, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_magrail', 'ig', 'Magrail', 'ig', 'Magrail', -0.30, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_brazos', 'ig', 'Brazos', 'ig', 'Brazos', -0.30, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_families', 'ig', 'Families', 'ig', 'Families', -0.02, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_edges', 'ig', 'Edges', 'ig', 'Edges', -0.02, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_sharks', 'ig', 'Sharks', 'ig', 'Sharks', -0.02, { self_feedback: true }),\n]\n\nQ.prosperityIntraDistrictMeshDefault = PROSPERITY_INTRA_DISTRICT_DEFAULT\nQ.prosperityIgHomeDistrictLinks = PROSPERITY_IG_HOME_DISTRICT_LINKS\nQ.prosperityDocksTrade = PROSPERITY_DOCKS_TRADE\nQ.prosperityPittsCrime = PROSPERITY_PITTS_CRIME\nQ.prosperityDistrictIds = PROSPERITY_DISTRICT_IDS\nQ.prosperityIgEffectivePop = PROSPERITY_IG_EFFECTIVE_POP\nQ.prosperityBlockBaseline = PROSPERITY_BLOCK_BASELINE\nQ.prosperityIgStart = PROSPERITY_IG_START\nQ.prosperityClampMin = PROSPERITY_CLAMP_MIN\nQ.prosperityClampMax = PROSPERITY_CLAMP_MAX\n\n/** IG prosperity defaults (root @start); block baseline lives in election_runtime. */\nfunction initEconomyProsperity() {\n    if (Q.prosperity_soylent == null) {\n        Q.prosperity_soylent = PROSPERITY_IG_START.Soylent\n    }\n    if (Q.prosperity_magrail == null) {\n        Q.prosperity_magrail = PROSPERITY_IG_START.Magrail\n    }\n    if (Q.prosperity_brazos == null) {\n        Q.prosperity_brazos = Number(Q.prosperity_soylent)\n    if (!isNaN(ps)) {\n        Q.stock_soylent = Math.round(142.9 * ps / scale * 10) / 10\n    }\n    var pm = Number(Q.prosperity_magrail)\n    if (!isNaN(pm)) {\n        Q.stock_magrail = Math.round(98.8 * pm / scale * 10) / 10\n    }\n    var pb = Number(Q.prosperity_brazos)\n    if (!isNaN(pb)) {\n        Q.stock_brazos = Math.round(201.3 * pb / scale * 10) / 10\n    }\n    var pf = Number(Q.prosperity_families)\n    if (!isNaN(pf)) {\n        Q.cloud_9_street_price = Math.round(34.3 * pf / scale * 10) / 10\n    }\n    var pe = Number(Q.prosperity_edges)\n    if Math.round(89.6 * pk / scale)\n    }\n}\n\n// might want to put some spending inertia into this system later on\n\n\nfunction organize_tick(){\n    Q.protest_good += Q.non_violence -1.5 // hurt corp trade_level_tick(){\n    if (Q.phase == 1) { Q.trade_level *= 1.0016667 }\n    if (Q.phase == 2) { Q.trade_level *= 0.9994444 }\n    if (Q.phase == 3) { Q.trade_level *= 0.9983333 }\n    if (Q.phase == 4) { Q.trade_level *= 0.9994444 }\n\n}\n\n// Tourism would also cover high end bespoke services like ship detailing\nfunction tourism_revenue() {\n    Q.overall_tourism = 2345 * Q.trade_level / (Q.crime_level * Q.crime_level)\n\n    Q.corp_tourism_rev = Q.overall_tourism * .41 + Q.overall_tourism * Q.trade_level\n    Q.affluent_tourism_rev = Q.overall_tourism * .39 - Q.overall_tourism * Q.trade_level\n    Q.coop_tourism_rev =  (Q.overall_tourism - Q.corp_tourism - Q.affluent_tourism)*.43\n    Q.poor_tourism_rev =  (Q.overall_tourism - Q.corp_tourism - Q.affluent_tourism)*.57\n}\n\nfunction longshore_revenue() {\n    Q.overall_longshore = 2678 * Q.trade_level / (Q.crime_level * Q.crime_level)\n\n    Q.coop_longshore_rev = Q.overall_longshore * .38 - Q.overall_longshore * Q.trade_level\n    Q.affluent_longshore_rev = Q.overall_longshore * .41 - Q.overall_longshore * Q.trade_level\n    Q.corp_longshore_rev =  (Q.overall_longshore - Q.corp_longshore - Q.affluent_longshore)*.31\n    Q.poor_longshore_rev =  (Q.overall_longshore - Q.corp_longshore - Q.affluent_longshore)*.33\n    Q.drone_longshore_rev =  (Q.overall_longshore - Q.corp_longshore - Q.affluent_longshore)*.36\n}\n\n// would be nice if there was a way to make drones shift to buying local as they get more independent minded\nfunction corp_purchase(){\n    corp_spend = Q.corp_money / 84\n    Q.corp_local_spend = corp_spend / Q.trade_level * .2\n    drone_spend = Q.drone_money * 0.60\n    Q.drone_local_spend = drone_spend / Q.trade_level * .4\n\n    Q.corp_capital_change = Q.corp_capital*.01 + (corp_spend *0.8 * Math.sqrt(Q.trade_level))\n\n    Q.corp_capital_revenue = corp_capital / 240\n}\n\nfunction coop_purchase(){\n    coop_spend = Q.coop_money / 36\n    Q.coop_spend\n\n}\n\n\nfunction economy_tick(){\n    trade_level_tick()\n    corp_purchase()\n}\n\n// Economic asset API: economic_assets_runtime.scene.dry\n\n\nvar CONTRACTING_VISITS_PER_PHASE = 2\n\n/** Grant 2 contracting draws per phase; unused visits bank for later phases. */\nfunction syncContractingAllowance() {\n    var phase = Number(Q.phase)\n    if (isNaN(phase) || phase < 1) { phase = 1 }\n    var through = Number(Q.contracting_allowance_through_phase)\n    if (isNaN(through) || through < 0) { through = 0 }\n    if (phase > through) {\n        var grant = (phase - through) * CONTRACTING_VISITS_PER_PHASE\n        Q.contracting_visits_remaining = (Number(Q.contracting_visits_remaining) || 0) + grant\n        Q.contracting_allowance_through_phase = phase\n    }\n    if (Q.contracting_visits_remaining == null || isNaN(Number(Q.contracting_visits_remaining))) {\n        Q.contracting_visits_remaining = 0\n    }\n    return Number(Q.contracting_visits_remaining) || 0\n}\n\nfunction monthly_tick_phase1() {\n    syncContractingAllowance()\n    organize_tick()\n    trade_level_tick()\n    economy_tick()\n    applyStratumProsperityBumps()\n    if (typeof Q.applyEconomicAssetProsperity === 'function') {\n        Q.applyEconomicAssetProsperity()\n    }\n    applyProsperityConnections()\n    if (typeof Q.processMonthlyHousing === 'function') {\n        Q.processMonthlyHousing()\n    }\n    if (typeof Q.tabulateBudget === 'function') {\n        Q.tabulateBudget()\n    }\n    syncProsperityDisplays()\n}\n\n/** Add amount to every voter block in one district; sync prosperity displays. */\nfunction bumpDistrictProsperity(districtId, amount) {\n    var state = Q.electionV2\n    if (!state || !state.blocks) { return false }\n    var bump = Number(amount)\n    if (isNaN(bump) || bump === 0) { return false }\n    for (var i = 0; i < state.blocks.length; i++) {\n        var block = state.blocks[i]\n        if (block.district.id !== districtId) { continue }\n        block.prosperity = clampProsperity(\n            (block.prosperity ?? PROSPERITY_BLOCK_BASELINE) + bump\n        )\n    }\n    if (typeof Q.qified_block_prosperity === 'function') {\n        Q.qified_block_prosperity()\n    }\n    if (typeof Q.syncStratumProsperityAverages === 'function') {\n        Q.syncStratumProsperityAverages()\n    }\n    return true\n}\n\nQ.monthly_tick_phase1 = monthly_tick_phase1\nQ.syncContractingAllowance = syncContractingAllowance\nQ.applyStratumProsperityBumps = applyStratumProsperityBumps\nQ.bumpDistrictProsperity = bumpDistrictProsperity\nQ.applyProsperityConnections = applyProsperityConnections\nQ.syncProsperityDisplays = syncProsperityDisplays\n\ninitEconomyProsperity()\nif (typeof Q.initEconomicAssets === 'function') { Q.initEconomicAssets() }\nsyncProsperityDisplays()\n\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes() }\n\n!}"

  "content": "title: The political situation\npriority: 1\nmax-visits: 1\ntags: event\non-arrival: {!\norganizing_unlock = 1;\nphase = 1;\nQ.contracting_allowance_through_phase = 0;\nQ.contracting_visits_remaining = 0;\nif (typeof Q.syncContractingAllowance === 'function') { Q.syncContractingAllowance(); }\n!}\n\nThe city government in Mikasa only holds substantial power in one section of the city: the Aurora district.  The \"big 3\" corporations run their home districts as fiefs.  The ganglands of the Pitts (the original mining district) are run by the syndicates to the extent they are run at all.  The wealthy Docks are controlled by a patchwork of private security forces. And finally the isolationist Deepers are stubbornly resistant to keep any and all influences of the city out.\n\nThe Aurora district holds the old capital building from which the Aurora Bank used to run Mikasa before it's sudden disappearance.  It's still nominally the government of the whole city but it's half abandoned due to both lack of funds and lack of need.  If we want to bring real democracy to Mikasa, we need to find cultivate a power base.\n\nThe local Aurora district council operates out of the same building and has elements of genuine democratic power.\n\n- @return"
}

## [2026-06-06T20:52:08Z] assistant (afterAgentResponse)

The contracting card is now limited to **2 views per phase**, with unused visits banking for later phases.

### How it works

- **`Q.contracting_visits_remaining`** — draws left this phase (and any banked from earlier phases).
- **`Q.contracting_allowance_through_phase`** — last phase that was already credited (+2 per phase gap).
- **`Q.syncContractingAllowance()`** in `organize_phase_tick.scene.dry` — when `phase` advances, grants `2 × (phase − through)` and updates `through`.

### Wiring

| When | What happens |
|------|----------------|
| **Organize phase start** | Resets counters, then sync grants **2** for phase 1 |
| **Monthly tick** (`monthly_tick_phase1`) | Sync runs first (catches phase bumps at month end) |
| **Main deck** (`main.scene.dry` on-arrival) | Sync before the deck is evaluated |
| **Contracting card** | `view-if: organizing_unlock = 1 and contracting_visits_remaining >= 1`; **on-arrival** decrements by 1 when the card opens |

Example: use it once in phase 1 → **1** banks. Phase 2 starts → sync adds **2** → **3** total available that phase.

`npm run lint:dendry` and `npm run make-html` both pass.

sqrt above 1). Higher → harder for Dems to claw back. */\nfunction effectiveParochialRadicalism(rad) {\n    if (isNaN(rad) || rad <= 0) { return 0 }\n    if (rad <= 1) { return rad }\n    return Math.sqrt(rad)\n}\n\n/** Share of derived xenophobia Dems can claw off H (1 − radicalism, floored at 0). */\nfunction demClawBackFraction(rad) {\n    return Math.max(0, 1 - effectiveParochialRadicalism(rad))\n}\n\n/**\n * Derive modifier stacks from Q.parochialism (0–100).\n * xenophobia_derived: ramps above Suspicion (41+). H+/C− on 1st/2nd via catalog.\n * xenophobia_clawed_back: derived * demClawBackFraction(rad); C still hit by derived only.\n */\nQ.syncParochialismXenophobiaStacks = function(q) {\n    q = q || Q\n    var p = Number(q.parochialism)\n    if (isNaN(p)) { p = 0 }\n    var rad = Number(q.parochial_radicalism)\n    if (isNaN(rad)) { rad = 0.65 }\n    var clawMult = demClawBackFraction(rad)\n\n    var suspicionFloor = 41\n    var maxStack = 20\n    var raw = p <= suspicionFloor ? 0 : ((p - suspicionFloor) / (100 - suspicionFloor)) * maxStack\n\n    q.xenophobia_derived = raw\n    q.xenophobia_clawed_back = raw * clawMult\n}\n\nQ.applyElectorateModifiers = function() {\n    var state = Q.electionV2\n    if (!state) { return }\n\n    Q.syncParochialismXenophobiaStacks(Q)\n\n    state.demographicsById = state.demographicsById || Object.fromEntries(\n        state.demographics.map(function(d) { return [d.id, d] })\n    )\n    state.districtsById = state.districtsById || Object.fromEntries(\n        state.districts.map(function(d) { return [d.id, d] })\n    )\n\n    // Districts are not rebuilt by syncAllVoteAppeals; reset from baseline each poll.\n    for (var j = 0; j < state.districts.length; j++) {\n        var dist = state.districts[j]\n        dist.competitiveScores = electorateCopyScores(\n            dist._baseCompetitiveScores || dist.competitiveScores\n        )\n    }\n    // Strata/global already base+appeals from election runtime sync; add stack deltas only.\n\n    for (var m = 0; m < Q.electorateModifierCatalog.length; m++) {\n        Q.electorateModifierCatalog[m].apply(state, Q)\n    }\n}\n// MIKASA_ELECTORATE_RUNTIME_END\n\nelectorate_runtime_ready = 1\n\n!}\ngo-to: jumpScene\n"

## [2026-06-06T21:04:07Z] assistant_thought (afterAgentThought)

A navigation issue may exist. Go-to commands referencing sibling subscenes from within a card subscene could fail.

The parser likely treats `@fair_result` subscenes as children of `@fair_bidding` rather than siblings. This structural misalignment breaks navigation to those subscenes.

Dendry treats all `@subscene` lines at the same indentation as siblings under the card root, so order does not affect nesting. A new line 71 error claims "Invalid property definition" on a prose line, likely caused by an em-dash in a title breaking the parser.

The go-to command may be failing to parse, leaving the parser stuck in property mode and causing the prose line to trigger an invalid property error. The target scene might need a full scene prefix such as `contracting.fair_result_coop_pitts`. Alternatively, splitting the logic into a single result subscene with conditional prose blocks would avoid the complex go-to line entirely.

## [2026-06-06T21:04:20Z] assistant (afterAgentResponse)

Added **Have honest and fair bidding** as a top-level contracting choice.

### Behavior

**Priority queue** (first open slot wins):
1. Coops — Pitts  
2. Brazos — Limelight  
3. Magrail — Railyard  
4. Soylent — Vats  
5. Coops — Aurora  
6. Local wealthy — Docks  
7. Local wealthy — Aurora  

A slot counts as taken if either the manual contract flag (`contract_coop_pitts`, etc.) or the fair-bid flag (`contract_fair_coop_pitts`, etc.) is already set.

**When a slot is awarded:**
- Same asset as the matching manual branch  
- Budget **2** (like outside corps)  
- Prosperity **+0.01** in **all seven districts**  
- Poll effects via new `contract_fair_*` stacks at **half** the manual deltas (`electorate_runtime.scene.dry`)  
- Navigates to `@fair_result` with flavor text for that winner  

**When the queue is exhausted:**
- No asset  
- Budget **1**  
- Prosperity **+0.02** citywide (double the normal fair-bid bump)  
- Exhausted-flavor text in `@fair_result`

Manual favoritism paths are unchanged; fair bidding uses separate `contract_fair_*` flags so you don’t double-count opinion effects.

## [2026-06-06T21:19:55Z] assistant (afterAgentResponse)

Yes — and you’ve already got the right shape in the file.

`fair_contract_uses` is incremented at the end of `@fair_bidding` `on-arrival` (line 67), **before** `go-to: fair_result`, so when `@fair_result` renders:

- **First visit:** `fair_contract_uses = 1` → intro prose shows  
- **Later visits:** `2`, `3`, … → intro prose hidden  

That’s cleaner than a separate `first_fair_contract` flag, since it’s one counter doing both jobs: tracking how many times fair bidding ran, and gating the first-time flavor text with `fair_contract_uses = 1`.

The only reason to keep a separate flag would be if you wanted the intro to show on a *later* visit under some other condition (e.g. after a reset). For “first time only,” `fair_contract_uses` is the right tool.

  "content": "title: Contracts and suppliers\nnew-page: true\nis-card: true\ncard-image: img/asteroid.jpg\ntags: local_organizing, city_affairs\nview-if: organizing_unlock = 1 and contracting_visits_remaining >= 1\non-arrival: {!\nif (typeof Q.syncContractingAllowance === 'function') { Q.syncContractingAllowance(); }\nvar contractingLeft = Number(Q.contracting_visits_remaining) || 0;\nif (contractingLeft > 0) { Q.contracting_visits_remaining = contractingLeft - 1; }\nvar fairChainPairs = [\n    ['contract_coop_pitts', 'contract_fair_coop_pitts'],\n    ['contract_big3_limelight', 'contract_fair_big3_limelight'],\n    ['contract_big3_railyard', 'contract_fair_big3_railyard'],\n    ['contract_big3_vats', 'contract_fair_big3_vats'],\n    ['contract_coop_aurora', 'contract_fair_coop_aurora'],\n    ['contract_wealthy_docks', 'contract_fair_wealthy_docks'],\n    ['contract_wealthy_aurora', 'contract_fair_wealthy_aurora']\n];\nvar fairChainComplete = 1;\nfor (var fc = 0; fc < fairChainPairs.length; fc++) {\n    if ((Number(Q[fairChainPairs[fc][0]]) || 0) < 1 && (Number(Q[fairChainPairs[fc][1]]) || 0) < 1) {\n        fairChainComplete = 0;\n        break;\n    }\n}\nQ.contract_fair_chain_complete = fairChainComplete;\n!}\n\nMikasa government contracts are a swamp of favor trading and graft.  By revoking old contracts we can make spending more efficient... and have the opportunity to make our own network of favors.  How should we develop these contracts?\n\n- @outside_corporations\n- @fair_bidding: Have honest and fair bidding\n- @big_3\n- @local_wealthy\n- @cooperatives\n\n@fair_bidding\ntitle: Have honest and fair bidding\nsubtitle: [? if contract_fair_chain_complete = 1: We've implemented technocratic awards ?][? if contract_fair_chain_complete = 0: Open scoring; award the next eligible local bidder ?]\non-arrival: {!\nvar fairUses = Number(Q.fair_contract_uses) || 0;\nvar MIKASA_DISTRICTS = ['Docks', 'Aurora', 'Vats', 'Railyard', 'Pitts', 'Limelight', 'Deeps'];\nfunction fairSlotOpen(reg, fair) {\n    return (Number(Q[reg]) || 0) < 1 && (Number(Q[fair]) || 0) < 1;\n}\nvar fairSlots = [\n    { n: 1, reg: 'contract_coop_pitts', fair: 'contract_fair_coop_pitts', asset: { id: 'pitts_coop_contract_workshop', name: 'Pitts coop guild workshop', district: 'Pitts', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'coop', kind: 'workshop', major: false } },\n    { n: 2, reg: 'contract_big3_limelight', fair: 'contract_fair_big3_limelight', asset: { id: 'limelight_brazos_contract_studio', name: 'Brazos municipal contract studio', district: 'Limelight', ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Brazos', kind: 'factory', major: false } },\n    { n: 3, reg: 'contract_big3_railyard', fair: 'contract_fair_big3_railyard', asset: { id: 'railyard_magrail_contract_depot', name: 'Magrail municipal contract depot', district: 'Railyard', ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Magrail', kind: 'factory', major: false } },\n    { n: 4, reg: 'contract_big3_vats', fair: 'contract_fair_big3_vats', asset: { id: 'vats_soylent_contract_yard', name: 'Soylent municipal contract yard', district: 'Vats', ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Soylent', kind: 'factory', major: false } },\n    { n: 5, reg: 'contract_coop_aurora', fair: 'contract_fair_coop_aurora', asset: { id: 'aurora_coop_contract_workshop', name: 'Aurora coop contract workshop', district: 'Aurora', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'coop', kind: 'workshop', major: false } },\n    { n: 6, reg: 'contract_wealthy_docks', fair: 'contract_fair_wealthy_docks', asset: { id: 'docks_wealthy_contract_office', name: 'Docks shareholder contract office', district: 'Docks', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'rich', kind: 'office', major: false } },\n    { n: 7, reg: 'contract_wealthy_aurora', fair: 'contract_fair_wealthy_aurora', asset: { id: 'aurora_wealthy_contract_office', name: 'Aurora boutique contract office', district: 'Aurora', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'rich', kind: 'office', major: false } }\n];\nvar picked = null;\nfor (var i = 0; i < fairSlots.length; i++) {\n    if (fairSlotOpen(fairSlots[i].reg, fairSlots[i].fair)) { picked = fairSlots[i]; break; }\n}\nQ.contract_fair_slot = 0;\nif (picked) {\n    Q.contract_fair_slot = picked.n;\n    Q[picked.fair] = 1;\n    if (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') { Q.addAsset(picked.asset); }\n    if (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') {\n        for (var d = 0; d < MIKASA_DISTRICTS.length; d++) {\n            Q.bumpDistrictProsperity(MIKASA_DISTRICTS[d], 0.01);\n        }\n    }\n    if (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') {\n        Q.queueBudgetExpense(2, 'contract_award', 'Fair-bid municipal contracts');\n    }\n} else {\n    Q.contract_fair_exhausted = (Number(Q.contract_fair_exhausted) || 0) + 1;\n    if (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') {\n        for (var d2 = 0; d2 < MIKASA_DISTRICTS.length; d2++) {\n            Q.bumpDistrictProsperity(MIKASA_DISTRICTS[d2], 0.02);\n        }\n    }\n    if (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') {\n        Q.queueBudgetExpense(1, 'contract_award', 'Fair-bid contract administration');\n    }\n}\nQ.fair_contract_uses = fairUses + 1;\nvar fairChainCompleteAfter = 1;\nfor (var fc2 = 0; fc2 < fairSlots.length; fc2++) {\n    if (!fairSlotOpen(fairSlots[fc2].reg, fairSlots[fc2].fair)) { continue; }\n    fairChainCompleteAfter = 0;\n    break;\n}\nQ.contract_fair_chain_complete = fairChainCompleteAfter ? 1 : 0;\n!}\ngo-to: fair_result\n\n@fair_result\ntitle: Fair bid award\n\n[? if fair_contract_uses = 1: We make the bidding process more honest the Caeser's wife.  Bidding parties have to send a representative to submit a bid on old fashioned paper at a closed room meeting for contracts that are awarded by Dutch auction.  Agreeing to rigorous audits is a requirement for entry. ?]\n\n[? if contract_fair_slot = 1: Surprisingly the coop out of the Pitts do very well in the first round of contracts.  The contracts are a lifeline for them and they make aggressively cheap bids. ?]\n\n\n[? if contract_fair_slot = 2: Technocratic reviewers rank Brazos Limelight studios highest on price, safety, and past performance.  The award is posted to the municipal ledger before lobbyists can rewrite the shortlist.  Brazos gets the contract studio; rivals grumble but the can't find any irregularities. ?]\n\n[? if contract_fair_slot = 3: Magrail's Railyard depot bids beat the field on logistics and bond backing.  Freight-adjacent contract labor will expand on schedule — chosen by spreadsheet, not by which executive bought dinner last week. ?]\n\n[? if contract_fair_slot = 4: Community Soylent's Vats compound posts the winning composite score.  Compound managers accept the yard order with strained grace; the public sees a megacorp win that at least survived open metrics. ?]\n\n[? if contract_fair_slot = 5: Aurora coop yards edge out the remaining bidders on fabrication capacity and worker retention.  Guild brokers citywide note that municipal business went to a local workshop on merit — a modest precedent, even if the margins are thin. ?]\n\n[? if contract_fair_slot = 6: Docks rentier workshops top the neutral rubric on solvency and subcontractor depth.  Shareholders pocket the prime line items while the award memo stresses audit trails over patronage. ?]\n\n[? if contract_fair_slot = 7: A local magnate does well in the bidding this round.  A little surprisingly, they plan an expansion in Aurora not the docks. ?]\n\n[? if contract_fair_slot = 0: At this point fair and transparent bidding is a well established tradition.  In the increasingly competitive market, contracts are getting awarded throughout the city with no area in particular profiting. ?]\n\n@outside_corporations\nview-if: contract_fair_chain_complete = 0\ntitle: Outside corporation suppliers\nsubtitle: They offer competitive pricing\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_outside += 1;\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') {\n    Q.queueBudgetExpense(2, 'contract_award', 'Outside supplier contracts');\n}\n!}\n\nWe steer contracts toward offstation suppliers with no permanent Mikasa footprint.  The Big 3 are a little annoyed but would be much angrier if they realized this lets us cultivate outside relationships that could be used against them one day.\n\n@big_3\ntitle: Big Three suppliers\n\nCommunity Soylent, Magrail Combine, and Brazos Zaibatsu all want the paperwork routed their way.  We can split the award across the neutral Docks hub — calming everyone a little — or concentrate work in one corp's home district and make a real friend there.\n\n- @big_3_docks: Split contracts through the Docks hub\n- @big_3_vats: Concentrate in the Vats (Community Soylent)\n- @big_3_railyard: Concentrate in the Railyard (Magrail Combine)\n- @big_3_limelight: Concentrate in the Limelight (Brazos Zaibatsu)\n\n@big_3_docks\ntitle: Split contracts through the Docks hub\nsubtitle: Spread the work; lower antagonism with all three megacorps\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_big3_docks = 1;\nsoylent_reprisal = Math.max(0, (Number(Q.soylent_reprisal) || 0) - 1);\nmagrail_reprisal = Math.max(0, (Number(Q.magrail_reprisal) || 0) - 1);\nbrazos_reprisal = Math.max(0, (Number(Q.brazos_reprisal) || 0) - 1);\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'docks_big3_contract_hub', name: 'Docks trilateral contract hub', district: 'Docks', ownerType: 'public', owner: 'city', kind: 'office', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Docks', 0.06); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes(); }\n!}\n\nLongshore clerks in the Docks process a three-way split.  None of the megacorps are thrilled, but none can claim we played favorites.  Trade clerks and corporate drones both see a little more steady work.\n\n@big_3_vats\ntitle: Concentrate in the Vats\nsubtitle: Favor Community Soylent; antagonize Magrail and Brazos\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_big3_vats = 1;\nQ.soylent_leverage = (Number(Q.soylent_leverage) || 0) + 1;\nQ.magrail_reprisal = (Number(Q.magrail_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nQ.brazos_reprisal = (Number(Q.brazos_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'vats_soylent_contract_yard', name: 'Soylent municipal contract yard', district: 'Vats', ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Soylent', kind: 'factory', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Vats', 0.08); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes(); }\n!}\n\nSoylent compound managers take victory laps while rival corp liaisons file angry protests.  Vats employment ticks up; drone households tied to Soylent notice the difference first.\n\n@big_3_railyard\ntitle: Concentrate in the Railyard\nsubtitle: Favor Magrail Combine; antagonize Soylent and Brazos\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_big3_railyard = 1;\nQ.magrail_leverage = (Number(Q.magrail_leverage) || 0) + 1;\nQ.soylent_reprisal = (Number(Q.soylent_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nQ.brazos_reprisal = (Number(Q.brazos_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'railyard_magrail_contract_depot', name: 'Magrail municipal contract depot', district: 'Railyard', ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Magrail', kind: 'factory', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Railyard', 0.08); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes(); }\n!}\n\nMagrail foremen expand shift schedules; Soylent and Brazos quietly route their complaints through syndicate channels.  The Railyard hums with freight-adjacent contract labor.\n\n@big_3_limelight\ntitle: Concentrate in the Limelight\nsubtitle: Favor Brazos Zaibatsu; antagonize Soylent and Magrail\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_big3_limelight = 1;\nQ.brazos_leverage = (Number(Q.brazos_leverage) || 0) + 1;\nQ.soylent_reprisal = (Number(Q.soylent_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nQ.magrail_reprisal = (Number(Q.magrail_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'limelight_brazos_contract_studio', name: 'Brazos municipal contract studio', district: 'Limelight', ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Brazos', kind: 'factory', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Limelight', 0.08); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes(); }\n!}\n\nBrazos showcase halls pick up maintenance and fit-out work.  The other two megacorps treat the award as a deliberate snub — useful leverage if we meant to pick a side.\n\n@local_wealthy\ntitle: Local wealthy contractors\n\nShareholder-adjacent workshops and Docks rentiers both bid for the same municipal line items.  Either cluster works; the city's moneyed strata gossip about the award no matter which district hosts the signing ceremony.\n\n- @wealthy_docks: Award through Docks firms\n- @wealthy_aurora: Award through Aurora firms\n\n@wealthy_docks\ntitle: Award through Docks firms\nsubtitle: Prosperity in the Docks; shareholders citywide take notice\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_wealthy_docks = 1;\nQ.rich_bump = 0.05;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'docks_wealthy_contract_office', name: 'Docks shareholder contract office', district: 'Docks', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'rich', kind: 'office', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.applyStratumProsperityBumps === 'function') { Q.applyStratumProsperityBumps(); }\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Docks', 0.05); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\n!}\n\nDocks shareholders pocket the prime subcontracts while their cousins in other districts still benefit from the spending ripple.  The old money crowd approves of government that pays on time.\n\n@wealthy_aurora\ntitle: Award through Aurora firms\nsubtitle: Prosperity in Aurora; shareholders citywide take notice\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_wealthy_aurora = 1;\nQ.rich_bump = 0.05;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'aurora_wealthy_contract_office', name: 'Aurora boutique contract office', district: 'Aurora', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'rich', kind: 'office', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.applyStratumProsperityBumps === 'function') { Q.applyStratumProsperityBumps(); }\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Aurora', 0.05); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\n!}\n\nAurora's boutique fabricators and logistics brokers land the contracts.  Wealthy households across Mikasa still feel the downstream orders — and remember who signed the checks.\n\n@cooperatives\ntitle: Local cooperatives and guilds\n\nCooperative workshops and guild ateliers can absorb small-batch municipal orders if we place the work where they already live.  Every site gives the local guilds a little more clout citywide.\n\n- @coop_aurora: Aurora cooperatives\n- @coop_pitts: Pitts cooperatives and Seven Families workshops\n- @coop_deeps: Deeps cooperatives\n- @coop_docks: Docks cooperatives and longshore guilds\n\n@coop_aurora\ntitle: Aurora cooperatives\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_coop_aurora = 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'aurora_coop_contract_workshop', name: 'Aurora coop contract workshop', district: 'Aurora', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'coop', kind: 'workshop', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Aurora', 0.06); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\n!}\n\nAurora coop yards pick up fabrication and life-support maintenance orders.  Guild brokers in other districts note that municipal business is no longer reserved for megacorp vendors.\n\n@coop_pitts\ntitle: Pitts cooperatives and guild workshops\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_coop_pitts = 1;\nQ.families_leverage = (Number(Q.families_leverage) || 0) + 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'pitts_coop_contract_workshop', name: 'Pitts coop guild workshop', district: 'Pitts', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'coop', kind: 'workshop', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Pitts', 0.07); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes(); }\n!}\n\nPitts cooperative machine shops and syndicate-tolerant guild floors share the workload.  The Seven Families treat the contract as recognition; coop members treat it as proof the city can hire locally.\n\n@coop_deeps\ntitle: Deeps cooperatives\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_coop_deeps = 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'deeps_coop_contract_workshop', name: 'Deeps sealed contract workshop', district: 'Deeps', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'coop', kind: 'workshop', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Deeps', 0.06); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\n!}\n\nDeepers' insulated workshops take contracts on their own terms — sealed bids, air-gapped ledgers, no corp middleware.  The district's prosperity tick rises without much fanfare outside the Deeps.\n\n@coop_docks\ntitle: Docks cooperatives and longshore guilds\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_coop_docks = 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'docks_coop_contract_workshop', name: 'Docks longshore coop workshop', district: 'Docks', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'coop', kind: 'workshop', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Docks', 0.05); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\n!}\n\nLongshore guild halls and Docks coop yards split harbor-adjacent orders.  Dockside prosperity inches up; guild fixers elsewhere file the precedent away for next quarter's negotiations.\n"
}

## [2026-06-06T21:23:37Z] assistant (afterAgentResponse)

When all seven fair-bid categories are filled (by fair bidding or the matching manual branches), `contract_fair_chain_complete` is set to `1`.

### What changes at that point

- **Hidden** (`view-if: contract_fair_chain_complete = 0`): Outside corporations, Big Three, Local wealthy, Cooperatives  
- **Still available**: Have honest and fair bidding (for the exhausted-administration visits)  
- **Subtitle** on fair bidding switches from “Open scoring…” to **“We've implemented technocratic awards”**

`contract_fair_chain_complete` is synced on each card open and again after a fair-bid award, so manual awards that fill the last open slot also lock out the patronage paths on the next visit.
